## 2026-10-23 — S3/F1 Activity membership prerequisite

[The prerequisite contract](docs/TAIL_SELECTION_CUSTODY.md) records the existing
capture/result-page boundary, a new bounded Go membership primitive, the proposed
tail publication/retry contract and a clearly planned user storyboard/diagram.
The primitive is unwired: no SSE field, endpoint, UI retention control or retry
journal was enabled. Existing v1 retained manifests still reject a tail origin.

`TailMembershipSnapshots` freezes server read provenance and canonical occurrence
payloads, with an opaque capability and expiry anchored to the original read.
Resolution is all-or-nothing across 1..20 references from one connection/selector;
it preserves each member's window, read clock, basis and late/limited diagnostics.
Input/output mutation, changed selectors/connections, duplicate occurrence aliases,
missing members, expired proof, delayed reissuance and restart cannot substitute
new evidence. Count/byte capacity refuses new issuance without evicting valid proof.
This is ephemeral observation membership, not durable custody or physical event ID.

Actual checks:
- Go exploration, retention and Go HTTP suites passed. Initial checks caught a
  helper naming error and a reserved Quint action name; both were corrected.
  A sandboxed broad tail check could not bind its HTTP test listener; the
  authorized test-server run passed. Final focused membership checks passed.
- Membership unit tests cover exact large numeric tokens, duplicate payload
  occurrences, per-poll clocks, late/limited flags, immutable copies, malformed/
  oversized batches, count/byte bounds, scope, selection bounds, expiry and restart.
- Go race detection passed concurrent issuance/resolution and all membership cases.
- Hegel membership state/swarm passed 70 generated sequences with 30 steps and TTL
  invariants after every action; existing tail overlap/multiplicity properties passed.
- Observed Quint issue/resolve/expire/refuse replay passed, with four controls
  rejecting forged counts, expired availability, member windows and accepting
  unavailable proof. This model does not claim an unimplemented durable publisher.
- Three real local XTDB integration checks passed: interrupted publication/retry,
  concurrent request recovery, and recovery of an already committed selection after
  fixed-page expiry. They verify existing saved/result-page origins only. Other
  optional ClickHouse/XTDB integration opt-ins in the broad suite were skipped,
  not counted as passes.
- The existing real-OTel live-tail journey 66 passed in 1.3 minutes, including its
  observed Quint replay and 30-second Bombadil campaign. The production tail UI/
  transport remains unchanged; frames 66a/b/c were refreshed from the running lab.
- Legacy HTTP rejects tail-reference fields and client rows. The v1 manifest guard
  rejects a self-consistent hash with a fabricated tail origin.
- OpenAPI conformance passed: 58 Go operations, 222 response fixtures, 56 admitted
  requests, 14 mux refusals, negative/SSE controls. No public wire schema changed.

Compact logs are in `artifacts/tail-membership-contract`; current live-tail
ledgers/Bombadil evidence remain with journey 66. New public functionality is not
advertised from this prerequisite. No runtime rebuild is needed for the unwired
bank; current docs/storyboards are synced separately. Owned scratch logs are
archived and cleaned; no telemetry or retained evidence is removed.

Next: implement a distinct typed tail manifest/idempotent durable publisher and
origin-aware resolution, preserving old receipt bytes. Only after committed retry,
changed-intent, expiry, outage and concurrency checks pass should SSE-issued
references and the browser selection/retry workflow be connected.

## 2026-10-23 — typed tail evidence and durable backend publication

Implemented `retention.TailIntent`, `TailManifest`, `TailReceipt`, `Store.PublishTail`
and the explicit `LoadTail` origin reader. They are library APIs only: no new HTTP
route, SSE capability, browser action, shared catalog/citation or correction support.
The v1 capture/result-page schema, hashes, tables and strict origin guards remain
unchanged. Dedicated tail tables atomically hold the manifest and request receipt;
initial annotation and custody clock live inside that immutable receipt.

Actual checks:

- `go test ./retention ./exploration ./cmd/goo -count=1 -v` passed with
  `GOO_XTDB_TEST_URL` pointing at the disposable local XTDB on port 15432.
  All retention integration tests ran, including legacy catalog/pinned documents,
  correction/read basis, interrupted/concurrent publication and expired fixed-page
  recovery. The optional ClickHouse source transport integration test was skipped.
- Native tail integration proves no partial manifest after an interrupted transaction,
  actual post-commit acknowledgement loss, recovery with no bank and after expiry,
  unchanged durable identities/clocks, changed note/tags/connection/selector/member
  set conflicts, absent expired proof refusal and concurrent receipt convergence.
  A connection outage is distinct from proof expiry. `LoadTail` succeeds while the
  existing v1 reader refuses the new origin.
- `go test -race ./retention ./exploration -run 'Test(Tail|XTDBTail)' -count=1 -v`
  passed. After tightening reference syntax and extending conflict cases, the
  retention subset was run again under `-race` and passed.
- In the separate verification module,
  `go test -run 'Test(TailManifestProperties|QuintObservedTailPublication|HegelTailMembershipStateSwarm|QuintObservedTailMembership)$' -v -count=1`
  passed with the real XTDB opt-in. Hegel runs 50 durable-manifest property examples
  and 70 membership state/swarm sequences. Observed Quint replay exercises real
  publication/recovery/conflict/expired-absence outcomes with four rejecting controls;
  membership replay retains its four independent controls.
- Canonical member validation rejects invalid provenance even after recomputing

  Normalization owns request data before sorting; no caller slice is mutated.
- `git diff --check` passed. No public wire contract or presentation changed, so no

## Activity membership, exact selection and retry integration — 2026-10-23

Implemented one startup-injected membership bank shared by trusted SSE issuance
and `POST /retained-evidence/tail`. Resets identify the original connection;
nonempty batches either issue frozen capability metadata or explicitly report
unavailable preservation while observations continue. Browser snapshots retain
per-row connection, read window/read clock, limited/late state and expiry.
Selecting pauses the stream and reveals compact controls for up to 20 occurrences;
Resume replacement clears selection without rebinding references. Publication
accepts only references and journals immutable intent before sending. Submitted
retry remains available after expiry/reload; payload edits are locked after send.

Executed checks:

- `go test -race ./cmd/goo ./exploration ./retention` passed with real local XTDB.
  New native HTTP tests prove actual source-poll issuance, exact numeric tokens,
  connection mismatch refusal, strict input guards, committed recovery with no
  bank after restart, changed-intent conflict and absent-proof 410. A final focused
  race run also proves exhausted issuance capacity keeps streaming observations
  without advertising a token.
- Hegel membership state/swarm and durable manifest properties passed, alongside
  observed Quint membership and actual XTDB publication replay with rejecting
  controls. Two focused Node files passed for bounded selection/provenance,
  expiry/connection/scope refusal, buffer independence and immutable journal reload.

  duplicates, FIFO limits, focused inspection, pause/resume and reconnect semantics.
  Journey 139 uses two actual OTLP log occurrences in ClickHouse through the
  primary compact Activity entry, selects both, resumes/clears, selects again,

- The resulting browser ledger passed observed Quint replay; five controls reject
  extra source reads, duplicate publication count, changed custody time, changed
  connection and altered member provenance. Bombadil's 25-second real browser run
  selects, clears and resumes without publishing or generating workload; bounded
  selection, pause state, overflow and exception invariants passed. Screenshots
  139a–c and affected live-tail frames were regenerated from the running app.
- OpenAPI conformance passed: 61 Go operations / 67 total, 222 frozen responses,

## Bounded Activity retention failure acceptance — 2026-10-23

[Acceptance contract](docs/ACTIVITY_RETENTION_ACCEPTANCE.md) now closes the bounded
S3/F1 interaction. Shared pure `GooRetentionStatus` distinguishes unsent local
failure, unknown commit outcome, unconfigured retention, refusal/conflict and
server-confirmed unavailable uncommitted proof. The client preserves HTTP status
and pre-send refusal metadata without changing journal bodies. Activity explains
configuration/expiry and the existing required tag-or-note contract before sending;
rows remain inspectable when proof is unavailable. Check retention only reads
configuration. Periodic expiry updates preserve unchanged live text and the
committed link DOM; retry/clear preserve keyboard focus. Old selection completions
cannot replace a newer selection's status. Saved recovery shares the status policy
but does not promise held observations after reload.

Executed checks:

- Six real browser scenarios (140–145) passed in 1.4 minutes: browser-clock expiry
  with no publication, actual unconfigured owner/failed check/recheck, real XTDB
  TCP transport failure, local journal quota refusal, genuine 64-batch capacity
  exhaustion and actual Go restart losing uncommitted proof. Retry after browser
  expiry sends unchanged intent and succeeds against restored real storage while
  server proof remains valid. Source reads after paused selection remain zero.
  The final Saved-context wording/mobile keyboard test was repeated and passed.
- The instances run the actual Go CLI and Python proxy over independently checked
  OTLP/ClickHouse records. A TCP forwarder breaks only their XTDB connection; no
  successful SSE/publication payload is mocked. Only a failed configuration GET
  and local storage quota are browser fault injections. Owned children, sockets
  and temporary stores close in `finally`. Initial browser checks exposed and
  corrected the new asset's missing Python allowlist entry and an empty annotation
  submission that was refused before reaching storage.
- Hegel's actual membership state/swarm (70 histories × 30 steps) and observed
  membership Quint replay passed. The observed 142 browser outage/retry ledger
  passes Quint replay; five controls reject false 410, hidden source reads,
  disabled submitted retry, changed refs and publication before acknowledgment.
  Its expiry/pending/retry fields come from browser observations. Bombadil's
  25-second capacity-refused inspection campaign passed its no-selection,
  no-overflow, inspection-progress and exception invariants.
- Four focused Node test files passed; Python docs handler's two checks and syntax
  checks passed. Existing OpenAPI conformance remains 61 Go / 67 total operations,
  222 frozen responses, 56 admitted requests, 14 mux refusals and SSE controls.
  The final deployed API docs browser check passed all 67 operations, desktop
  capture, mobile layout and no page errors. Five deployed UI assets match the
  checkout byte-for-byte.

Screenshots 140–145 and the acceptance diagram, README, current plan, known issues,
component ownership and storyboards are synchronized. Compact checks live in
`artifacts/activity-retention-acceptance`; observed per-scenario ledgers, verification
logs and Bombadil output live under `artifacts/tail-acceptance`.

The final query/packages image is
`sha256:a430e567509531187c791750802292295b25abcc8ec3a5f8bf8d0f1c0a1ea570`.
Only query/packages are recreated. Intermediate image/tag and unused regular build

Conflict/refusal messages have unit coverage, without claiming a browser

  aligned UTC/service and inline actions with >=28 px targets. 701/1024 retain
  table-row display. No measured width overflows the document.
- Keyboard occurrence selection and inspection retain original exact timestamp,
  selected occurrence, opener focus and paused parent through fixed-question
  staging/return. Added qualified fields, hidden Service, field refinement and
  removing a draft add no source read. Explicit Resume supplies the second source
  start and a disclosed gap. quint-go validates observed events with the existing
  entry model and all five rejecting controls.
- 160's 25-second Bombadil campaign passes at an explicitly configured 390×844
  viewport. Its actual width/row geometry, bounded rows, paused selection/inspection,
  progress and <=one source start are checked. The shared campaign runner accepts
  an optional validated width/height pair and records it only when supplied; old
  callers retain their default flags/result shape.
- Synthetic passive contract 161 passes after correcting the stylesheet collision:
  SSR and detached/reinserted SPA records, long values, exact focusable timestamps,
  exposed column headers, keyboard action listeners and zero API requests across
  the same five widths. Application styles deliberately load after component
  styles. Tables without the opt-in retain the legacy mobile layout.
- 161, real record-column journey 118, its SSR/property-upgrade check and real
  entry/scroll/backgrounding journeys 102–103 pass together (five tests, 1.6m).
  Actual mobile/desktop frames, component reference, README, design/ownership flow,
  storyboard, plan and known issues are synchronized. Journey 109 also passes
  (43.4s), refreshing the existing navigation frames while retaining source-free
  draft/editor identity, focus, its observed model controls and default-size
  Bombadil behavior.

This is a display change. It adds no query, source adapter, selection/preservation
protocol, saved configuration field, clock or subscription. The initial passive
check correctly rejected the old application's stylesheet override; that conflict
was fixed by separating the layouts rather than accepting the stacked result.
No successful integration responses were mocked in the new 160/161 checks. Existing
103 retains its explicit synthetic 429 transport refusal.

Local deployment updates static assets/reference only; native binary and Docker
images are unchanged. Application telemetry, captures, XTDB, volumes and reusable
caches are preserved. Compact browser/model results remain in
artifacts/mobile-records-round and the per-journey directories.

Long arbitrary values and extra fields may expand rows. This cut accepts compact
Activity rows, not fixed-height virtualization; fixed/saved tables retain their
existing layout until separately adopted.

## Compact fixed/saved record adoption — 2026-10-24

into the compact responsive record layout. `mobileCompact` is independent from

with a short mobile rendition and full accessible timestamp in the same exact
`time` element. Context buttons show short text while retaining exact accessible
IDs/tooltips, including inspector follow-up actions. Spanning inspector rows stay
full-width. Legacy telemetry-card projection excludes opt-in tables.

  The supplied-record Hegel campaign passes 200 cases over the actual JavaScript
  presenter, retaining exact time, durations and bounded reported ancestry (0.25s).
- New 162 log/span journeys each ingest four ordinary records of both signals,
  independently counted in ClickHouse. Fixed links stage before Run; fixed and
  saved rows are checked at 320/390/700/701/1024 px for grid/table transitions,
  context actions, short/full times, full-width captions and document containment.
- Saved reopen, long-message expansion, checkbox selection, spanning inspection,
  fields and hiding Service add no source read. Exact selected event time,
  original question and evidence basis remain in the inspector. The original
  receipt and complete captured result compare equal after interaction. Explicit
  Expand trace adds exactly two existing context reads in the original fixed
  bounds; the linked-log table adopts the same layout, and Close restores focus.
- Both new saved-view Bombadil campaigns pass at 390×844 for 25 seconds: selection,
  inspect/close, column disclosure/hiding and selection preservation stay source-free
  with compact rows, full-width detail, containment, progress and no uncaught errors.
- Synthetic 161 now covers population-styled fixed tables, responsive full/short
  dates, preserved long-message disclosure and spanning detail rows with app styles
  loaded last. It retains the standalone SSR/SPA/legacy checks and zero API reads.
- Final 162 log/span, 161, record-field 118 and SSR/property-upgrade, and shared
  live-to-fixed presentation 106 pass together (six tests, 2.2m). Source-free
  presentation equality and captured/saved field layouts remain intact.
- Existing saved-owner journey 107 passes on the final assets (15.5s), refreshing
  its saved/context frames and replaying actual read/history events through
  quint-go with four rejecting controls. Its delayed actual context response
  still cannot replace a different capture.
- Screenshot inspection identified a narrow caption and inherited tail preview
  height. The final component fixes use full-width captions and unbounded expanded

No query, saved schema, evidence receipt, API operation, native executable or
Docker image changes. Deployment updates static assets only. Telemetry, stored

Long records and expanded details remain variable-height. Service-held span tables
retain their existing presentation pending their own adoption.

No query, source adapter, operation accounting, saved schema, custody protocol or
native executable changes. The existing saved-operation journey restarts only the

under artifacts/record-renderer-audit-round and per-journey directories. Successful
integration data is real; transport refusals and committed-response loss remain
explicit negative tests.

The remaining prioritized semantic finding is explicit: normalized operation
waterfall rows derive their StatusCode display from a declared operation outcome.
Separating that domain outcome from raw reported status needs its own compatible
contract change; this formatting audit does not silently alter that mapping.


## Independent operation status — 2026-10-24

Operation queries now retain optional exact exporter status separately from
manifest-declared outcome. Legacy projections still omit the new field. The
waterfall and cached inspector label both facts; only raw reported Error colors
a bar. See [contract, diagram and storyboard](docs/OPERATION_STATUS.md).

Actual checks:
- `go test ./annotations ./investigations ./cmd/goo` passed, including the
  exhaustive 18-case absent/empty/known/future status × declared-outcome matrix,
  basis/conflict checks and frozen legacy JSON.
- Node operation/visualization suites passed; Hegel exercised the actual JS
  projection with generated independent statuses/outcomes.
- Journey 164 passed using genuine previous/current native executables and six
  OTLP spans, an independent ClickHouse oracle, an immutable old capture,
  one explicit fixed-question refresh and source-free local inspection.
- Observed Quint replay passed with five rejecting controls: hidden restore
  read, fabricated legacy status, wrong retained status, wrong error color and
  changed fixed bounds. The 25-second mobile Bombadil run passed, exercising
  six trace selections and cached inspectors.
- Six deployed browser regressions passed: heatmap drilldown (09), scoped subject
  events (10), durable saved refresh (19), and three standalone component
  contracts covering HTML upgrade, detached SPA updates, row-index facts,
  literal text, cloned inputs, accessible facts and malformed-data refusal.
- Generated OpenAPI passed 64 operations, 222 response fixtures, 56 requests,
  14 mux refusals, negative controls and SSE checks. Deployed API contract passed
  matching schema plus seven real handler responses, without publishing records.

The first Hegel invocation hit the read-only default home cache; configuring the
workspace cache resolved that environment issue. Its initial generated fixture
also omitted required duration data; the fixture was corrected and the property
passed. Neither was a product regression. Native HTTP tests require local socket
access. The old/new compatibility test is explicitly opt-in via the two binary
paths documented above; missing prerequisites report a skip.

The local packages executable and UI/schema assets are upgraded without rebuilding
Docker images. Existing telemetry, receipts, XTDB and volumes are preserved.
Compact ledgers/results/logs are under artifacts/operation-status and
artifacts/operation-status-round; the fixture’s disposable owner is removed.
Screenshots 09/10/19/67 and new 164 frames reflect the running UI.


## OTel-aware observed field discovery — 2026-10-24

[OTel field hints](docs/OTEL_FIELD_HINTS.md) now enrich supplied observations,
field datalists, counted headings and cached attribute inspectors through one
advisory display catalog. Exact qualified keys, literal values, legacy spellings
and query/source ownership remain intact. Original exporter-key search remains
available alongside names and descriptions. No source query, API or saved schema
was added.

Actual checks:
- Twenty Node tests across field hints, field wire fixtures, attribute inspection,
  filter validation and observed suggestions passed. They cover exact namespaces,
  detached metadata, unknown lookalikes, unchanged legacy predicates, original
  exporter-key search and refusal to coerce status attributes into numeric filters.
- `TestHegelOTelFieldHints` passed with generated namespace/key/value combinations
  against actual JavaScript. Recognizing a key never grows the supplied field
  list, rewrites predicates or changes missing/empty/string semantics.
- Real-data journey 165 passed over current, legacy and custom OTLP spans with an
  independent ClickHouse membership oracle. Activity pauses/browses/stages;
  fixed questions inspect and explicitly group/filter; fixed population edits
  stage children. The original bounds and exact legacy keys survive execution.
- The existing Quint staging replay passed with four rejecting mutations for a
  hidden read, changed predicate, changed window and changed environment. The
  25-second mobile Bombadil actor performed one explicit read, found both HTTP
  method spellings and staged the exact legacy key without another source read.
- The synthetic detached-adapter browser contract passed: optional metadata,
  no emitted edit on supplied updates, cloned input, retained disclosure/focus,
  keyboard selection and exact grouping callback intent without API requests.
- Existing real attribute-inspection (29), observed-suggestion (150) and counted-value (151) journeys passed,
  including failed/obsolete reads, missing/empty values, saved reopening and
  source-free child staging.

Screenshots 165 document the new workflow; affected existing storyboards are
regenerated from the local UI. The field catalog is a bounded advisory subset
checked against the dependency's cached OTel registries, not a schema validator,
backend convention resolver, migration engine or full semantic-convention catalog.
Raw exporter status does not become business/HTTP outcome policy.

Only static UI/docs assets change in the local deployment. Native executable and
Docker images are unchanged. Test owners remove their disposable stores; existing
application telemetry, receipts, XTDB and volumes are preserved. Compact ledgers,
Quint logs and Bombadil outcomes are under artifacts/otel-field-hints; Node results
are under artifacts/otel-field-hints-round.

stage a child for one signal. Predicates, clocks, exact keys, companion questions
and saved parent configurations stay intact. Explicit Run executes the query.

## Compose two grouping fields — 2026-10-24

[Two-field composition](docs/COMPOSED_GROUPING.md) adds exact ordered Add/remove
intent and controlled chips without a new query grammar. Grouping-only drafts
retain scoped observations from their originating read; fresh reads, changed
scope and saved reopen withdraw that sample. Unread population children update
in place, preserving the applied parent and companion questions.

Actual checks:
- Five Node suites passed across grouping transforms, field wire presentation,
  filter validation, suggestions and OTel hints. The new composition case covers

  paging reset, split-timeline clearing and detached parent/companion data.

  over add/remove/replace/refusal and detached mutations. Original nested filters,
  exact clocks, bucket width and independent companion groupings remain intact.
- Real-data journey 167 passed over four OTLP spans and a companion log. Activity
  and fixed analysis compose before Run; a saved two-signal view opens one child
  and edits its unread draft in place. UI save/reopen preserves both fields but
  no observation sample. Exactly seven explicit source requests occur. An

- `TestQuintComposedGroupingLedger` accepted observed read/query traces and rejected

  and wrong aggregate count. The 25-second mobile Bombadil actor reads once and

- The controlled synthetic browser contract passed for keyboard Add, duplicate
  and capacity guards, exact callback modes, chip removal to zero, unchanged

- Existing population-workspace journey 91 passed against the deployed UI,

Initial browser test attempts targeted controls inside unopened save/field

Only completed passing runs above are acceptance evidence.
